| pe62:PE-62.1 | READ WHAT THE CLERK LEFT OUT (tools/pe62_c1.py). Random classifier search (random feature groups: numerals, rounding, name string, first/last sign, position, neighbours' classes, tablet size, tablet system, header, museum batch; logistic / naive Bayes / extra trees; ~5,200 hypotheses) trained on the 3,489 entries whose class sign is written (sign deleted), top-15 ensembles judged on 30% held-out tablets with the class masked. NULLS: labels shuffled across / within tablets, numerals shuffled. CONTROLS: proto-cuneiform and Ur III with class signs/words deleted at PE size; planted name-borne and x5-number implicit classes | PE 0.672 acc / 0.95 bits (label shuffle <= 0.29 / 0.00; within-tablet shuffle 0.60-0.63 / 0.76-0.79; numbers shuffled 0.60 / 0.71-0.73); number-blind 0.60 / 0.74; copy-the-tablet 0.585. PC 0.65 / 0.81, Ur III 0.91 / 1.97 (number-blind over within-tablet shuffle: Ur III +0.17-0.25 bits, PC +0.04-0.07, PE -0.01 to -0.05). Plants: name-borne recovered at >= 45 tokens (recall 0.94, 0.47; Ur III 0.81), ranked but not called at 27-30; x5 numbers 0.87-0.91 | B: masked classes come back, but from the tablet and the numerals; PE name strings hold no entry-level class information beyond the tablet (PC-like, not Ur III-like). |
| pe62:PE-62.2 | FILL IN THE GAPS (tools/pe62_c2.py): impute all 4,745 unmarked entries (data/pe62_imputed_classes.tsv); number-blind imputation tested on (i) the entry's own numerals, (ii) totals on 18 tablets where capacity vs count reading matters, (iii) tablet number system. NULLS: imputations permuted across / within tablets (500x), coin (2,000x). CALIBRATION: masked labelled entries; PC; Ur III | 92% of fill-ins = tablet's commonest written class. Numerals: +0.073 bits vs within-tablet null +0.064 (gain +0.009); on labelled held-out PE -0.026, PC -0.003, Ur III +0.106 vs tablet nulls. Totals: 16/18 close with imputed choice = written-only choice 16, other 7, coin 11.5. System: 0.762 vs written 0.739 (null 0.728) | B (negative): filling in changes nothing that the written classes did not already fix; the clerk omitted what the tablet implied. |
| pe62:PE-62.3 | HIDDEN CARRIERS AND WHOLE-TABLET MASKING (tools/pe62_c3.py; c1 modes bnonbr/nonbr). Within-tablet class excess of each name sign (400 within-tablet permutations, BH), cross-tablet batch-matched null, DEV->HOLD replication, planted carrier; random search re-run with no class visible on the tablet. Controls PC, Ur III | PE 8 signs at q <= 0.10; 5/12 replicate (p 0.07; PC 7/17, Ur III 14/19 incl. oil/onion/grain words); plant found (q 0.04). Whole tablet masked, number-blind: PE 0.11 bits (nulls <= 0.00), PC 0.25, Ur III 1.25; PE with numerals 0.54. M056: only line-final (40) or before M288 (32); M056-final lines have the largest numbers of 61 final-sign groups | B-: weak carriers M056, M217 -> allotment; M259, M340, M390 -> person lines. B: tablets with no class sign leave 0.11 bits outside the numerals. C: 'M056 n' = M056 M288 written short (would kill: M056-final lines of 1-3 per person). |
| pe62:PE-62.1a | PE, numbers allowed. Nulls: training labels shuffled across tablets (x3), shuffled within each tablet (x4), numerals shuffled across entries with the tablet system shuffled across tablets (x3). | Held out (1,112 entries): acc 0.672, balanced 0.667, 0.950 bits/entry (first-150 ensemble 0.941); majority 0.246. Label shuffle max 0.254 / 0.001 bits. Within-tablet shuffle 0.603-0.633 / 0.755-0.792 bits. Number shuffle 0.600-0.605 / 0.707-0.730 bits. Copy the tablet's commonest written class: acc 0.585. Every top-15 hypothesis uses the neighbour group. | B: the masked class is readable (+0.95 bits), but ~0.8 bits of it is the tablet's own habit. What is left inside the entry (~0.15-0.18 bits over the within-tablet shuffle) is about the same as what the numerals add (~0.22 bits over the number shuffle). |
| pe62:PE-62.1b | PE, number-blind (numeral, rounding and tablet-system groups barred). | acc 0.601, balanced 0.539, 0.743 bits (first-150 0.738); this is the within-tablet shuffle level (0.76-0.79 bits with numbers) and only 1.6 points above copying the tablet. | B (negative): once the numbers are hidden, the name string, position, header and batch add almost nothing beyond the tablet's other entries. |
| pe62:PE-62.1c | Controls at PE size. PC (992 held out) and Ur III (1,193 held out), same search; label and within-tablet shuffles. | PC: 0.646 / 0.807 bits (blind 0.633 / 0.769); label shuffle (x2) max 0.353 / 0.018; within-tablet shuffle (x2) 0.616-0.621 / 0.698-0.728; copy-tablet 0.612. Ur III: 0.909 / 1.971 bits (blind 0.912 / 1.907); label shuffle (x2) max 0.275 / 0.008; within-tablet (x2) 0.791-0.833 / 1.654-1.737; copy-tablet 0.778. | Controls pass (well above the label shuffle). Number-blind against the within-tablet shuffle: Ur III +0.17 to +0.25 bits (acc 0.912 vs 0.79-0.83; copy-tablet 0.778), PC +0.04 to +0.07 bits, PE -0.01 to -0.05 bits. The method sees words that carry the class when they exist (Ur III). PC is close to PE: mostly the tablet. |
| pe62:PE-62.1d | Planted implicit classes. A: every labelled entry with a random name sign X (25-120 tokens, >= 5 tablets) gets class PLANT, with no class sign. B: 10% of labelled entries get class PLANT and x5 numerals. | PE A: M097 (30 tokens, 8 held out) recall 0.00, but mean P(PLANT) 0.19 on true vs 0.009 on others; M259 (46) recall 0.94, precision 1.00; M347 (48) recall 0.47, precision 1.00. PC A: SI4 (27, 4 held out) recall 0.00 (P 0.22 vs 0.015). Ur III A: 'ga' (54) recall 0.81, precision 1.00. PE B (x2): recall 0.91 / 0.87, precision 0.77 / 0.79. | Control passes for a name-borne class seen >= ~45 times and for a number-scale class; at ~30 tokens the plant is ranked but not called. So at PE size the search can find a hidden class carried by one name sign, if the sign recurs about 45+ times. |
| pe62:PE-62.2a | Fill-in. Ensembles retrained on all 3,489 labelled entries, applied to 4,745 unmarked ones. | With numerals: COUNTED 1,422, PERSON 1,545, MEASURED 914, ALLOT 794, FRACLINE 70; mean confidence 0.59; 821 at >= 0.8. Blind: mean confidence 0.57; 596 at >= 0.8. Agreement between the two 0.77. 92% of blind imputations equal the commonest written class on the same tablet (3,491 entries on tablets with a written class). 1,254 unmarked entries sit on tablets with no class sign at all. | C: the fill-in is mostly 'same as the tablet'. Table in data/pe62_imputed_classes.tsv. |
| pe62:PE-62.2b | Numeral consistency (blind imputation -> observed numeral key). Nulls: rows permuted across entries / within tablets. Calibration: masked labelled held-out entries (truth known), and PC. | PE unmarked (4,734 with numerals): +0.073 bits/entry; across-entry null -0.092 (p < 0.002); within-tablet null +0.064 (p < 0.002). By kind: name-only +0.050 (tablet null 0.048, p 0.016), bare +0.099 (0.097, p 0.04), inner class sign +0.090 (0.090, p 0.58). Calibration, PE labelled held out: +0.231 vs tablet null +0.257 (p 1.0); the true class would give +0.473. PC labelled held out: +0.143 vs tablet null +0.146 (p 0.79), true class +0.199; PC unmarked +0.050 vs +0.047 (p 0.006). Ur III labelled held out: +0.672 vs tablet null +0.566 (p < 0.002), true class +0.729; Ur III unmarked +0.163 vs +0.158 (p 0.08). | B (negative): the filled-in classes predict numerals only through the tablet. The entry-level gain on unmarked PE entries is +0.009 bits/entry: significant, but small, and even on labelled entries with known truth the method gains nothing at entry level (PE and PC alike). Calibrated: Ur III shows a clear entry-level gain (+0.106 bits over its tablet null), so the test can see it where words carry the class; PE (-0.026) and PC (-0.003) do not. |
| pe62:PE-62.2c | Totals. Tablets with one total and only ambiguous numerals (N01/N14/N45/N34) where capacity and count readings differ; system chosen by the blind class mix (MEASURED/ALLOT/FRACLINE -> capacity). Nulls: other choice; coin; written classes only. | 95 such tablets, 18 where the reading matters. Imputed choice closes 16, the other choice 7, a coin 11.5 (p 0.008). The written classes alone also close 16. Only one of the 18 (P008806) has no written class, and it closes either way. Misses: P008229 and P009370 close only as capacity, though their classes say counts (P008229 is one of pe42's five 'count' tablets that close in capacity). | B (negative for the fill-in): classes pick the right system for totals (16/18 vs coin 11.5), but this is the written classes. Filling in the gaps adds 0 closures. |
| pe62:PE-62.2d | Tablet number system (CAPT vs CNTT, 847 tablets) from class shares, logistic, tablet-grouped 5-fold. Null: imputed rows permuted (200x). Calibration: PC. | PE: majority 0.689; written classes 0.739; written + blind-imputed 0.762; permuted 0.728 (p < 0.005). PC: 0.842 in every condition (majority 0.842). | B: filling in raises the system prediction by 2.3 points over written classes alone (pe59's role layer was 72%; a plain sign classifier 77%). A real but small gain, and again tablet-level. |
| pe62:PE-62.3a | Hidden carriers, W and X tests, PE (80 name signs with >= 8 labelled tokens on >= 4 tablets). | W: 20 of 80 at p <= 0.05 (4 expected), 8 at q <= 0.10: M009 -> PERSON (n 58, O/E 1.26), M387 -> COUNTED (197, 1.25), M004 -> FRACLINE (73, 1.27), M056 -> ALLOT (33, 1.15), M203 -> PERSON (21, 1.64), M217 -> ALLOT (20, 1.39), M240 -> PERSON (20, 1.69), M340 -> PERSON (15, 1.71). X: 4 at q <= 0.10 (M056 -> ALLOT, pX 0.002). | B-: some name signs carry class information inside the tablet, but effects are small (O/E 1.15-1.7). |
| pe62:PE-62.3b | Controls and plant for 3a. | PC: 104 signs, 28 at p <= 0.05, 24 at q <= 0.10 (BAR, N57, GI, U2 -> PERSON; SZE3, ZABALAM -> ANIMAL; SI4, NE -> GRAIN). Ur III: 87 words, 29 at p <= 0.05, 25 at q <= 0.10 (naga, id-gur2, sza3-iri -> OIL; szum2, saga, lugal, dug -> GRAIN; numerals inside lines -> CATTLE). Plant (PE): |M131+M388| relabelled to MEASURED (14 of 17 tokens) found at pW 0.002, q 0.04, O/E 2.04. | Method passes: a planted carrier is found, and Ur III returns real commodity-bound words (oil, onion and grain words). |
| pe62:PE-62.3c | Replication on held-out tablets (DEV carriers at p <= 0.05, same top class on HOLD). | PE 5 of 12 (chance 0.2; binomial p 0.07): M056 -> ALLOT, M217 -> ALLOT, M259 -> PERSON, M340 -> PERSON, M390 -> PERSON; M004, M009, M203, M240, M387 flip. PC 7 of 17 (p 0.04). Ur III 14 of 19 (chance 0.14; p 8e-9). | B- (weak): PE carriers replicate at the edge of chance, less well than PC and far below Ur III. |
| pe62:PE-62.3d | Outside check: capacity numerals on unmarked entries carrying carriers, vs other unmarked entries on the same tablets. | PE: capacity-class carriers (65 entries) +0.001, count-class carriers (480) -0.015. PC +0.008 / +0.004; Ur III -0.027 / -0.026. M056 unmarked lines: -0.16 (n 16). | Null in all three corpora (the test has no power: no control passes it). Uninformative. |
| pe62:PE-62.3e | M056 follow-up (post hoc). Where does M056 sit, and how big are its numbers when the class sign is missing? Null: the 61 final-sign groups of unmarked lines with >= 10 count-readable numerals. | M056 occurs 73 times in entries: 40 at the end of the line, 32 directly before M288, 1 before M149; never elsewhere. With a class sign it is ALLOT 32 of 33. Unmarked M056-final lines have the largest numbers of all 61 final-sign groups (median 2^4.2 ~ 18 under count values; ALLOT 2^3.3, PERSON 2^0, COUNTED 2^1; vs ALLOT p 0.06, vs PERSON p < 0.001). Another M288 predecessor, M096 (27 before M288), has small numbers when unmarked (median 2^1.0, n 87). | C: 'M056 n' is an allotment line with M288 left out (M056 M288 written short). Rank 1 of 61 (p ~ 0.016, chosen after 3a). Would support: new M056-final lines in M288-allotment position with M288-sized values (60/120 N39C per unit of the preceding person line). Would kill: M056-final lines with count-sized values (1-3), or M056-final lines followed by M288 lines on the same tablet as separate allotments. |
| pe62:PE-62.3f | Whole-tablet masking (neighbour group barred), number-blind (300 hypotheses) and with numerals (300). Nulls: label shuffle (x2). Controls PC, Ur III blind (150 each) with label shuffle. | PE blind: acc 0.361, balanced 0.292, 0.107 bits (majority 0.246; nulls 0.224-0.289 / <= -0.002 bits). PE with numerals: 0.572 / 0.540 bits. PC blind: 0.452 / 0.255 bits (majority 0.355; null 0.355 / -0.005). Ur III blind: 0.748 / 1.252 bits (majority 0.309; null 0.273 / 0.008). Groups in PE's top blind models: name, position, header, batch. | B: on tablets that write no class sign at all, PE name strings, position, header and batch give 0.11 bits/entry of class information: real (above every null), but half of PC's and a tenth of Ur III's. With numerals it rises to 0.54 bits. For the 1,254 entries on tablets with no class sign, the class can be guessed only from the numbers. |
| pe62:PE-62 | Masked-class inference by random classifier search (tablet-grouped CV, top-15 ensembles, 30% held-out tablets); fill-in of unmarked entries; hidden-carrier tests. Nulls: labels shuffled (across tablets x3-5; within tablets x4), numerals shuffled (x3), imputations permuted (500x), coin for totals (2,000x), within-tablet label permutation for carriers (400x). Controls: proto-cuneiform and Ur III with class signs/words deleted at PE size (3,489 labelled entries); planted implicit classes (name-borne x3, number-scale x2); planted carrier. | Held out: PE 0.67 acc / 0.95 bits (label shuffle <= 0.29 / 0.00; within-tablet shuffle 0.60-0.63 / 0.76-0.79; number shuffle 0.60 / 0.71-0.73). Number-blind 0.60 / 0.74 = within-tablet shuffle level. Whole tablet masked, number-blind: PE 0.11 bits, PC 0.25, Ur III 1.25. Fill-in: 92% = the tablet's commonest written class; totals +0 closures (16/18 either way, coin 11.5); numeral gain beyond the tablet +0.009 bits/entry; system prediction +2.3 points (0.739 -> 0.762, null 0.728). Carriers: 8 PE name signs at q <= 0.10, 5 of 12 replicate on held-out tablets (p 0.07; PC 7/17, Ur III 14/19). Controls pass (Ur III entry-level +0.17-0.25 bits; plants of >= 45 tokens and x5 numbers recovered). | B (calibrated negative). The unwritten class can be read back, but almost all of it is the tablet's own class and the numerals. Name strings, position, header and batch add ~0.1 bits. Filling in the gaps changes no totals and barely moves the grammar. PE behaves like proto-cuneiform, not like Ur III. C: M056 = 'M056 M288' written without M288. |

| pe65:PE-65.1 | ONE STATE OR MANY OFFICES ACROSS IRAN (port of la58). Agent-based C simulator: 7 world kinds (state with random centre, capital + satellite offices, independent offices, temple estate, trading houses, mobile herding offices, none) x 33 nuisance parameters, paperwork written as opaque line strings + numbers + sealing, thinned to real unit counts (Susa in 3 publication batches, Yahya 27, Malyan 22, Sialk 12, Sofalin 11); 300,000 worlds; ABC-RF. CONTROLS: 8 planted kinds; 20 unit shuffles; Ur III and Linear B at exact PE shape | in-model institutions vs none 0.93; planted centres 2.5-3x background. PE: none + herders 0.16 (shuffles 0.46); P(Susa-centred state) 0.12 (shuffles 0.11, planted 0.31-0.41); centre flat. LB -> none (pass); Ur III -> herders (fail) | B: shared institutions, no centre located |
| pe65:PE-65.2 | Rejection ABC with held-out units (3 Susa batches, 4 outposts); held-out model choice by world kind. CONTROLS as above, planted Susa state / null / peers | Susa batches gain 0.50 (= shuffles); outposts 0.017 vs 0.06-0.17 shuffles, 0.37-0.55 controls, 0.24-0.34 planted states; no kind beats shuffles at predicting outposts | B: outposts not predictable from Susa |
| pe65:PE-65.3 | Model adequacy; Banesh-only (Yahya + Malyan) check; marker read-offs by sign knockout and by excess outpost sharing vs 1,000 shuffles. CONTROLS: Ur III titles, LB office words, planted capital + offices | PE inside the cloud (77th pct), Ur III outside (100th). Yahya + Malyan gain 0.011. Knockouts move nothing (M348 0.03); 0/295 strings shared beyond chance; read-off AUCs 0.27-0.57 on Ur III and planted | B negative: no marker sign; C: independent offices sharing script, not a Susa-centred state |
| pe65:PE-65.1a | Calibration on 4,000 held-out simulated worlds. | Type accuracy 0.53 (chance 0.14); institutions vs none 0.93. T, S, P, I, M confuse with each other; H and N confuse with each other (141 of 535 H read as N, 78 of 580 N as H). Centre AUC 0.84 for every site; Susa-centred state AUC 0.90 (bin 0.3-0.5 -> 58 % true; 0.1-0.3 -> 17 %). Parent accuracy 0.09-0.25 (chance ~0.17). | A (method): at PE size the paperwork separates 'shared institutions' from 'none or herders', places a centre moderately, and cannot tell which kind of hierarchy or who depends on whom. |
| pe65:PE-65.1b | Planted worlds on the PE frame (8 each, nuisance from the prior): Susa-centred state, Susa capital + offices, Yahya-centred state, peers (Susa, Yahya, Malyan independent), herding offices, null, Susa temple estate, trading houses. | Centre P: planted Susa 0.38-0.40 vs other sites 0.12-0.16; planted Yahya 0.48 vs 0.17-0.22. Susa-centred state P 0.31-0.41 when planted at Susa, 0.05 when planted at Yahya, 0.02 for herders/null. Type: N 0.51, H 0.38, M 0.37, S 0.41, I 0.29 (top in 4-6 of 8); T 0.20 (top 3/8); P 0.24 (top 4/8). | B (method): planted centres are recovered at 2.5-3x background and the Susa-state flag responds correctly; kinds of institution are only weakly separated. |
| pe65:PE-65.1c | Real PE (publication batches; also random Susa batches). Null: 20 unit-label shuffles (documents re-dealt over the 7 units, sizes kept). | Type: M 0.29, S 0.16, T 0.14, P 0.14, I 0.11, H 0.11, N 0.04 (random batches: M 0.26, N 0.05). Shuffles: N 0.12 (max 0.21), H 0.34 (max 0.49), M 0.15 (max 0.28). Centre: Yahya 0.23, Malyan 0.22, Susa 0.18, Sialk 0.16, Sofalin 0.16 (shuffles 0.10-0.12; planted Susa centre gives 0.38). Susa-centred state 0.12 (random batches 0.08; shuffles 0.11, max 0.16; planted 0.31-0.41). | B: the PE units share institutions (N + H 0.16 vs 0.46 in shuffles), the reverse of Linear A (la58: N 0.44). No site is read as the centre: Susa scores below a planted Susa centre and below Yahya and Malyan; P(Susa-centred state) is at shuffle level. Kind of institution: a weak lean to trading houses (M 0.29, at the shuffle maximum), C at best. |
| pe65:PE-65.1d | Control Linear B (truth: separate palace states). | N top in 5/5 replicates (0.28-0.48, mean 0.37), P 0.12, T 0.10; centres flat 0.16-0.18; Susa-slot state 0.10. | Control passes for 'no shared institution' (like la58), fails for locating the palaces. |
| pe65:PE-65.1e | Control Ur III (truth: one state; capital Ur, depot Puzrish-Dagan). | H (mobile herding offices) top in 5/5 (0.57), N 0.23, T + S 0.09; centres flat 0.08-0.11. | Control FAILS: one central state at PE shape reads as mobile offices with shared personnel and no centre. (Caveat: the Ur III rebuild keeps only tablets with >= 2 numbered lines, so it has no short notes.) |
| pe65:PE-65.2a | Rejection-ABC posterior, all statistics. Null: 5 unit shuffles. | PE: M 0.24, T 0.18, I 0.15, P 0.14, H 0.11, N 0.11, S 0.07; P(Susa-centred state) 0.10. Shuffles: N + H 0.21-0.47 (PE 0.21); Susa state 0.05-0.13. LB: N 0.38-0.46. Ur III: H 0.48-0.52, N 0.28-0.35. Planted Susa state: Susa-state 0.12-0.16; planted null: N 0.60-0.82. | B-: same picture as 1c with rejection: institutions lean, no Susa-centred state (0.10, inside shuffles and below planted 0.12-0.16). Kind of institution flat. |
| pe65:PE-65.2b | Held-out predictability: gain = prior error - posterior error, Susa batches vs outposts. | PE: Susa batches 0.50, outposts 0.017 (Yahya 0.11, Sofalin 0.05, Sialk -0.01, Malyan -0.09). Random batches: 0.51 / 0.016. Shuffles: 0.49-0.54 / 0.06-0.17. Ur III 0.69-0.78 / 0.52-0.55; LB 1.12-1.14 / 0.37-0.43; planted Susa state 0.10-0.35 / 0.24-0.34; planted peers 0.43-0.67 / 0.04-0.66; planted null 0.17-0.38 / 0.08-0.31. | B: the three Susa batches behave as one administration (as predictable as in shuffles), but the plateau outposts are almost unpredictable from the rest of the corpus: gain 0.017, below all 5 shuffles, all 6 control replicates and all 3 planted Susa states. Malyan is predicted worse than by random worlds. Caveat: Sialk and Sofalin are early, partly numerical tablets (period mixed with place). |
| pe65:PE-65.2c | Held-out model choice: N-world error minus best institution-world error at outposts (> 0: institutions help predict a held-out outpost). | PE 0.075 (random batches 0.079); shuffles 0.016-0.124 (mean 0.059); planted Susa state 0.12-0.37; planted null -0.06 to -0.11; LB -0.02 to -0.05 (separate states: correct sign); Ur III -0.09 to -0.12 (one state: WRONG sign). Best single kind for PE: M (0.585) vs T 0.599, P 0.589, N 0.641; shuffles also pick M (4/5). | Control half-fails (LB and planted pass, Ur III fails). PE sits inside the shuffle range: institution worlds help PE outposts only as much as they help shuffled paperwork. The M preference is generic (shuffles share it): killed as a reading. |
| pe65:PE-65.3a | Model adequacy: nearest-neighbour distance of each corpus panel to 150,000 bank worlds vs the NN distances of 500 held-out simulated worlds (percentile; > 0.95 = outside the cloud). | PE 0.77 (7.66 vs median 6.64); PE random batches 0.70; PE shuffled 0.35; Linear B 0.40; Ur III 1.00 (20.9 vs 6.6). | B: PE and Linear B lie inside the simulated cloud; Ur III lies far outside it, so the Ur III failures (1e, 2c) are extrapolation failures and Ur III is not a valid test of the method here. The method thus has no in-cloud 'one state' control except planted worlds. |
| pe65:PE-65.3b | Chronology check on 2b: held-out gain for the two Banesh-phase outposts only (Yahya + Malyan), dropping the early Sialk and Sofalin. | PE 0.011 (random batches 0.026); shuffles 0.11-0.17; Ur III 0.51-0.55; LB 0.34-0.58; planted Susa state 0.23-0.34; planted null 0.09-0.38; planted peers -0.04 to 0.67. | B: the outposts' idiosyncrasy is not an artefact of early tablets; Yahya and Malyan are as unpredictable from Susa as all four outposts together, below every shuffle and every planted Susa state. Planted peer worlds can reach it (-0.04), planted null worlds mostly not. |
| pe65:PE-65.3c | Knockout read-off: delete every line containing one sign (274 signs, >= 5 lines), recompute the panel, read the drop in P(institutions) and P(Susa-centred state). Controls: Ur III titles (17) and Linear B office words (4) at PE shape; planted Susa capital + offices (title and personnel words known). | PE: base P(institutions) 0.956; drops 5th-95th pct -0.001 to 0.000; only M348 moves it (0.030). Shared signs (87) and one-unit signs (187) do not differ (-0.0005 vs 0.0000). Control AUCs: Ur III 0.27, LB 0.42, planted 0.46 / 0.50 / 0.52. | Killed: single-sign knockouts do not move the posterior and the truth titles are not found in any control. M348 is not a marker on this evidence. |
| pe65:PE-65.3d | Excess-sharing read-off: for each line string with >= 3 tablets, the number of outposts it reaches (if also at Susa) vs 1,000 unit shuffles. Controls as 3c (3 replicates each); planted capital + offices, planted Susa state, planted null (3 each). | PE: 0 of 295 strings at p < 0.01; 254 of 295 reach fewer outposts than shuffling predicts (mean obs - exp -0.22). Top (p 0.11-0.20): M072, M222, M383, M054.M288, M221, M351, |M136+M365|. Control AUC: Ur III titles 0.42-0.45; LB 0.79-0.91 (2 titles only); planted capital + offices 0.52-0.57; planted Susa state 0.40-0.50. Every corpus, real or planted, shares less than shuffling (-0.06 to -0.32). | Killed as a marker detector (fails Ur III and planted worlds). Descriptive B: no PE sign string is shared between Susa and the outposts beyond chance; the outposts' vocabulary is local. |

| pe64:PE-64.1 | RATION LADDER, cycle 1 (tools/pe64_c1.py): ~1.02 M rate-detection rules per run (7 scopes incl. dossier neighbours and last line, M288/non-M288 targets, N01 lines read both ways, count >= 1-3, 61 source x 21 target signs, exact or not; 700 k random sign OR-sets). Discovery on one half, held-out on the other; swap null (capacity values of the same notation shape from other tablets) and within-tablet re-pair null. Controls: Ur III '-ta' ration lists with the rate hidden (full and 120 tablets), planted 20/40/80 ladder, swap corpus as real, proto-cuneiform | Ur III full: 19 rungs incl. 60/30/20/10/15/5/90/120; at PE size 60 both halves plus 1-2 lower rungs. Plant: 3/3 rungs both halves. Null as real: 1 false rung. Proto-cuneiform: nothing. PE: only 60 N39C validated (AB 10 tablets vs 3.5, p 0.005; BA p 0.03-0.04); 48, 72, 84, 4 single-direction at p <= 0.05 | one rung (the pe27 allotment); proto-cuneiform cannot calibrate this format |

PE count >= 2: 37 vs 46.8 and 50 vs 52.0 (same-shape null), beats only the free null. Nulls: false strict rung 1 of 6 runs. Power: 6/6, 1/6, 0/6 rungs at 10/5/3 tablets. | pe61:PE-61.final | EVERY BUREAUCRACY ON EARTH VOTES ON PROTO-ELAMITE (tools/pe61_common.py, pe61_build.py, pe61_c1.py, pe61_c2.py, pe61_c3.py, pe61_report.py, pe61_resid.py, pe61_report3.py; checkpoints data/pe61_ckpt/; reuses linear-a la57 corpora, features and survivor specs). PE (1,160 tablets, 20,300 tokens) reduced to la57's value-free form (opaque signs, numerals after signs, line breaks; 42 features on Linear-A-sized draws). Three arms: (A) la57's universal taggers trained on seven administrations incl. proto-cuneiform and khipus; (B) fresh taggers selected and trained with proto-cuneiform NEVER seen, PC then used as the held-out control; (C) a parliament, each system (and Linear A's logogram form, khipu top cords) voting with its own experts. Nulls: role labels permuted in training; PE shuffled within entries (S2), across all slots (S1), and line order (S3); dumb single-feature baselines; layout-covariate residuals. Controls: planted administration; PC held out; PC re-laid out in PE order. | Planted recovered (COM 0.86 / 0.96). PC held out: COM 0.572 per model vs null 0.44-0.50 (weak pass; unchanged in PE order 0.571). World commodity vote vs pe59 MEASURED+COUNTED: A 0.870, B 0.901 (S2 q95 0.886, S3 q95 0.879, null max 0.47), parliament 6 of 7 voters 0.74-0.92, 26 majority signs vs shuffles <= 19. Single feature 'rank of the adjacent number' alone 0.893 on the commodity-vs-PERSON contrast. Commodity-vs-PERSON split 0.910 with PC in training, 0.688 without (within shuffles). TOT: majority = label-permuted voters (18 vs 18). PER / PLA / TRA: none above shuffles; PER voters rank pe59 PERSON below chance. Arm A vs B commodity ranks rho 0.82. | NOT CRACKED. B: the world's commodity role finds pe59's MEASURED/COUNTED class-sign layer (independent support, from bureaucracies that never saw PE or its neighbour), but it is mainly a 'largest quantity follows me' detector, not a reading. A (calibrated negative): no outside bureaucracy finds a PE total sign, person, place or transaction, matching pe59's 'totals have no sign'. B (negative, leakage): only proto-cuneiform separates pe59 PERSON units from goods; the rest of the world counts them as goods. C: commodity/class-sign candidates missing from pe59: M269, M354, M367, M418, M482, M069, M206, M268, |M036+1(N30D)|, |M288+1(N01)| (kill: they head entries or sit in name strings at >= the PERSON rate on new tablets); M054 commodity 6/7 (C-). |

| pe75:PE-75.1 | Cycle verdict | No Mesopotamian-finger ruler in PE clay (planted 17 mm found at excess 7.6-18; real <= 1.9). A height-only ripple at 11.0 mm passes a frozen 3-part test (both museums, not line pitch, not width) but sits inside what Ur III catalogue widths can do by chance | B (negative): tablets were not cut to a ~17 mm unit. C: PE heights step at ~11 mm. Would support: an independent re-measurement (Louvre or Tehran own records, or photogrammetry) keeps the 11 mm excess >= 2.5, and PE tablets from the new excavations (Sofalin, Ozbaki) fall on the same lattice. Would kill: re-measured heights lose the ripple, or a shape-matched null tuned on Ur III widths (calibrated to give 5% false hits) leaves PE h below threshold |

| pe79:PE-79.0 | PRE-CHECK and PIVOT. Requested idea (universal role-taggers trained on khipu, proto-cuneiform, Ur III, Linear B..., scored by leave-one-civilisation-out transfer, applied to PE) was ALREADY RUN as linear-a la57 and PE pe61 (seven administrations incl. Open Khipu Repository khipus, LOSO, label-permuted nulls, planted administration). Quick web search: no published cross-civilisation graft of this kind (nearest: PE header sequence models, EMNLP 2022). Pivot to the inversion: PE is not tagged, it is the JUDGE. A PE-only structural grammar P(sign given slot context [, previous sign]) is learned from one random half of PE (570 tablets, damage-aware common.load via pe56 line form); known ledgers are reduced to role skeletons (role symbol per token, numerals moved after signs = PE entry order; khipu: each cord = one entry, top cords = TOT, other cords = COM) and GRAFTED onto PE: a hypothesis H puts one PE sign (of the top 120) in each role COM UNI PER PLA TRA HDR TOT; unknown-role tokens are wildcards. Score = role-balanced mean PMI of the grafted signs under the PE judge (how Proto-Elamite the transplanted ledger reads). tools/pe79_common.py, pe79_c1.py, pe79_report1.py; ckpt data/pe79_ckpt/. | Corpora: PE 1,140 tablets / 11,787 sign tokens; PC 3,827 docs, UR3 5,000, OB 4,082, EB 1,283, OA 290, LB 3,838, KH 605 (98 top cords). | Setup (pivot logged: requested idea = la57/pe61). |
| pe79:PE-79.1a | MASSIVE RANDOM GRAFTING: 48 PE-grammar architectures (numeral class full/collapsed x slot-in-line on/off x line-in-tablet on/off x previous-sign bigram on/off x smoothing 0.5/0.8/0.95) x 100,000 random grafts = 4.8 M hypotheses, each scored on 7 known ledgers + 3 label-permuted copies of each (types permuted within corpus; token level for khipu) + PLANT. PLANTED CONTROL: the other PE half with roles planted from sign identity (COM=M288, HDR=M157, UNI=M297, PER=M388, PLA=M387, TRA=M346, TOT=M003). | Planted graft ranks top in 48/48 architectures (percentile 0.9993-1.0000; z ~5). | PASS: the judge recognises a graft that matches PE's own layout. |
| pe79:PE-79.1b | LEAVE-ONE-CIVILISATION-OUT: rank grafts by mean z on 6 ledgers, keep top 500, mean percentile on the held-out ledger (real labels). NULL: same selection made on the 6 ledgers with role labels permuted (3 reps); held-out stays real. Trusted architecture = beats all nulls on EVERY held-out ledger incl. khipu AND recovers the planted graft. | Held-out percentile real vs null-max (medians over 48): UR3 0.965/0.817 (48/48 wins), OB 0.979/0.879 (48/48), EB 0.909/0.823 (48/48), OA 0.948/0.817 (48/48); PC 0.795/0.845 (12/48); LB 0.727/0.798 (9/48); KH 0.538/0.709 (0/48). Best: arch 06/07/09/30 win 6/7, all lose on KH. TRUSTED: 0 of 48. | KILL: no architecture transfers to khipu (0/48) and none to all seven. The four cuneiform ledgers agree among themselves, but they share one role lexicon (la57 MESO_W), so that agreement is leakage, not a universal. |
| pe79:PE-79.1c | FAMILY HOLD-OUT (the strict version: train on the other families only): CUN = PC+UR3+OB+EB+OA held out (train LB+KH), AEG = LB (train CUN+KH), AND = KH (train CUN+LB). Same nulls. | CUN real median 0.617 vs null-max 0.714 (6/48 wins); AEG 0.727 vs 0.798 (9/48); AND 0.538 vs 0.709 (0/48). Architectures winning all three families: 0/48. | A (calibrated negative): grafted onto Proto-Elamite grammar, the world's ledgers share no transferable role layout; what selection buys on unseen families is general 'PE-likeness' of signs, equal under permuted labels. Same conclusion as la57/pe61 (no universal total; persons/places do not transfer), now with PE as judge. |
| pe79:PE-79.2a | PER-SIGN GRAFT ROLES (tools/pe79_c2.py). No architecture was trusted (79.1b), so all 48 are averaged and the result is graded as untrusted. Marginal effect of putting PE sign s in role r on ledger k = mean z over the ~830 random grafts with H[r]=s; role-specific part d = real-label marginal minus mean of 3 label-permuted marginals, averaged over ledgers having the role (>= 30 tokens). NULL: permuted copy 1 vs mean of copies 2-3, same arithmetic; a sign is called for a role if d exceeds the null's 99th percentile. Frozen BEFORE comparison: data/pe79_roles_A.tsv, sha256 cc73cb1f586c91ab6c6050bfdedb1fbfa9981feb37bfa4deaa35bb00f6f1f557 (120 signs x 7 roles). | Top COM: M260, M122, M069, M354, M006, M362, M217, M243 (7/7 ledgers same sign). UNI: M122, M260, M069, M217, M314, M003, |M218+M288|, |M106+M288|. TOT (incl. khipu): M122, M125, |M218+M288|, M217, M003. HDR: M080, M103, M254, M041, M145, M305. PER: |M136+M365|, M329, M348, M139. | Graft roles exist above the permuted-label null for every role, and the same signs come up on most ledgers; the column for 'commodity', 'unit' and 'total' is one set of signs (M122, M260, M217, M003). |
| pe79:PE-79.2b | COMPARISON with established PE (tools/pe79_compare.py), after freezing. Sign-set AUC on the frozen d column; perm-label null column; 2,000 random sign sets; single-feature slot baselines (sign's line-final rate, header-line rate, last-line rate). | pe59 MEAS+CNT as COM 0.774 (perm-label null 0.701, p < 0.001 vs random sets, but line-final baseline alone 0.878); as UNI 0.627. pe59 PERSON as PER 0.540 (null 0.566). PREFIX as PER 0.327. Header signs (M157, M327; n 2) as HDR 0.754 (header-rate baseline 0.996); M157 is called PLA #4, HDR #38. M288 (per-head allotment): COM #36, UNI #33 of 120, no role. M153 last-line compounds: not among the 120 graftable signs; M153 itself TOT #19. COM column vs sign's header rate rho -0.77, vs line-final rate +0.64; HDR/PER/PLA/TRA columns all rho +0.41-0.52 with header rate. | B (negative): the graft roles are layout. COM/UNI/TOT = 'sits at the end of an entry line', the other four = 'sits in a header line', and each loses to its one-feature slot baseline. They do not recover the per-head rule (M288 unranked), the header signs (M157 lands in PLA), or a total sign (pe59 A 'totals have no sign' stands). C-: M122, M260, M217 as the signs PE grammar most readily lends to the world's commodity/unit/total slots (would support: half-B judge gives the same top signs and they beat the line-final baseline; would kill: rank change across halves or no gain over baseline). |
| pe79:PE-79.3a | REPLICATION: the whole pipeline rerun with the PE judge trained on the OTHER half (B; PLANT = half A). 48 architectures x 100,000 grafts. Frozen data/pe79_roles_B.tsv, sha256 8f51f8753547ecc72bc01792c376e0ea83eb3f93a2ab7210f17178cfb5e111e9. Stability of per-sign role columns A vs B (Spearman, 98 shared signs) against the same statistic on the permuted-label null columns. | Planted graft top in 48/48. Trusted architectures 0/48; KH held out wins 0/48; family hold-out CUN 7/48, AEG 10/48, AND 0/48, all-three 0. A vs B rho: COM 0.36, UNI 0.18, PER 0.09, PLA 0.26, TRA 0.19, HDR 0.35, TOT 0.28; null-vs-null 0.39, 0.31, 0.45, 0.18, 0.38, 0.14, 0.75. Leads: M260 COM #3 / UNI #1 / TOT #1 in B (A: COM #1, UNI #2); M122 and M069 drop out of B's 120 graftable signs; M217 COM #75. | The negative replicates. Role columns are no more stable across halves than the null columns. M122/M217/M069 killed; only M260 repeats. |
| pe79:PE-79.3b | KILL CONTROL ON THE JUDGE: PE half A with signs shuffled over all slots (S1) as the judge, same 48 x 100,000 grafts. | Planted graft recovered in only 8/48 (the judge is blind, as it should be). Yet the cuneiform ledgers 'transfer' exactly as before: UR3/OB/EB/OA held out 48/48 wins each (real 0.91-0.98 vs null-max 0.78-0.83); KH 0/48; all-three families 0. Lead ranks under S: M260 COM #46 / UNI #64 / TOT #98; M069 UNI #2 (spurious). | Decisive kill: the cross-ledger 'transfer' inside cuneiform does not need PE grammar at all; it is the shared la57 role lexicon plus frequency. The cycle-1 within-cuneiform wins are leakage (A). |
| pe79:PE-79.3c | LAYOUT RESIDUAL: COM and UNI role columns regressed on each sign's header-line rate, line-final rate, last-line rate and log frequency; residual AUC for pe59 MEAS+CNT. Judges A, B, S. | A: COM raw 0.774 -> residual 0.485; UNI 0.627 -> 0.431. B: COM 0.706 -> 0.429; UNI 0.593 -> 0.425. S: 0.399 / 0.408 (raw = residual). | Nothing beyond layout: once slot rates are removed the graft roles carry no information about PE's class signs (B, negative). |
| pe79:PE-79.final | GRAFT THE WORLD'S LEDGERS ONTO PROTO-ELAMITE (tools/pe79_common.py, pe79_c1.py, pe79_report1.py, pe79_c2.py, pe79_compare.py, pe79_c3.py; ckpt data/pe79_ckpt/; frozen data/pe79_roles_A.tsv sha256 cc73cb1f586c91ab6c6050bfdedb1fbfa9981feb37bfa4deaa35bb00f6f1f557, data/pe79_roles_B.tsv sha256 8f51f8753547ecc72bc01792c376e0ea83eb3f93a2ab7210f17178cfb5e111e9). PIVOT: the requested universal role-tagger with leave-one-civilisation-out incl. khipu was already la57/pe61, so the problem was inverted: PE grammar (learned from PE only) is the judge, and role skeletons of proto-cuneiform, Ur III, OB, Ebla, Old Assyrian, Linear B and Inca khipus are grafted onto it by random role->PE-sign maps. 3 judges (half A, half B, half A sign-shuffled) x 48 grammar architectures x 100,000 random grafts = 14.4 M hypotheses, each scored on 7 ledgers, 21 label-permuted ledgers and a planted PE corpus. Leave-one-ledger-out and leave-one-family-out (cuneiform / Aegean / Andean) transfer. | Planted graft recovered 48/48 (A, B), 8/48 with the shuffled judge. Trusted architectures (transfer to every held-out ledger incl. khipu): 0/144. Khipu held out: 0/144 wins. Family hold-out, all three families: 0/144. Cuneiform-to-cuneiform transfer 48/48 under every judge INCLUDING the shuffled one (leakage). Frozen per-sign roles vs established PE: pe59 MEAS+CNT as COM 0.774 (line-final baseline 0.878; residual after slot rates 0.485 / 0.429 on B); PERSON as PER 0.54 (null 0.57); header signs as HDR 0.75 (header-rate baseline 0.996; M157 lands in PLA); M288 unranked (COM #36); M153 compounds not graftable (outside the top 120). A vs B role stability no higher than the null's. Only M260 repeats (COM/UNI/TOT top 3 in A and B; #46-98 under the shuffled judge). | NOT CRACKED. A (calibrated negative): there is no universal grammar of bookkeeping that carries from khipu, Linear B and cuneiform into Proto-Elamite; with PE as judge, every ledger's roles reduce to 'end of an entry line' vs 'header line', and that alone beats the grafts. A: the within-cuneiform agreement in la57-style corpora is leakage (it survives a sign-shuffled PE judge). B (negative): the graft roles add nothing to pe59's class signs, the per-head M288 rule, the headers or M153's last line; 'totals have no sign' (pe59 A) stands. C-: M260 is the sign PE grammar most readily lends to the world's commodity/unit/total slots (would support: on new tablets M260 lines end entries and carry the tablet's largest capacity number more often than M297/M036 do; would kill: it stays at the capacity-family rate, or drops out of the top 10 under a third random PE split). |

## Summary

See FINDINGS.md for the state of knowledge before 4 Oct 2026.
